Title: Historia Fratrum Bohemorum, eorum ordo et disciplina ecclesiastica ad ecclesiae recte
  constituendae exemplar, cum Ecclesiae Bohemicae ad Anglicanam paraenesi (with the Panegersia);
  praefatio by Johann Franz Buddeus
Creator: Jan Amos Comenius (last bishop of the Unity of the Brethren); Johann Franz Buddeus
  (preface)
Publisher: Halae: typis et impensis Orphanotrophii, 1702
Publication Date: 1702
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: Printed at Halle in 1702 (title page); public domain by the project's date rule
  (published 1930 or earlier). On Internet Archive, the item metadata carries no rights field; that
  tag is supporting evidence only. The scan is a Google Books digitisation hosted on Internet
  Archive; the usage request in its front matter does not claim rights in the public-domain work.
  Checked 2026-09-30.
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_WoZLAAAAcAAJ (archive.org/details/bub_gb_WoZLAAAAcAAJ),
  full-text OCR file bub_gb_WoZLAAAAcAAJ_djvu.txt, fetched directly (curl, raw bytes) 2026-09-30 by
  the Library thread; the body below is that file unaltered.
Provenance note: Body unaltered, including its own OCR errors. Header prepended by the Library
  thread. The scan is a Google Books digitisation hosted on Internet Archive. A second scan of the
  same printing is bub_gb_dmdTAAAAcAAJ; this file is the fuller of the two.
Content note: The Unity's later account of its own history, order and discipline, written by its
  last bishop and printed with Buddeus's preface.
